| Id | Export | Correction | Restriction | Erasure |

| ingestion-anonymisation-eligibility-control | include-digests-and-blocker-in-authorized-case-ledger | re-evaluate-from-current-owner-projections | fail-closed-on-missing-stale-or-unsupported-evidence | authorize-only-the-exact-approved-current-source-graph |
| ingestion-anonymisation-restore-control | include-minimum-owner-proof-in-controller-authorized-tenant-export-excluding-plans-and-keyed-fingerprints | replay-only-from-the-protected-data-rights-ledger | keep-the-source-identity-barrier-fail-closed | cryptographically-delete-fingerprint-keys-after-tenant-lifecycle |
| ingestion-tenant-destruction-proof-control | include-in-authorized-tenant-termination-audit | append-a-corrective-control-plane-record | retain-only-the-minimum-owner-proof | remove-after-approved-proof-retention |

| reservation-history-rights | include-with-authorized-export | append-correction | restrict-processing | redact-after-retention |
